2026-09-11T17:23:26.4493654Z # This file is used to manage the dependencies of the Chromium src repo. It is
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4494499Z # used by gclient to determine what version of each dependency to check out, and
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4495174Z # where.
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4495510Z #
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4495996Z # For more information, please refer to the official documentation:
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4496937Z #   https://sites.google.com/a/chromium.org/dev/developers/how-tos/get-the-code
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4497653Z #
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4498155Z # When adding a new dependency, please update the top-level .gitignore file
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4498894Z # to list the dependency's destination directory.
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4499375Z #
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4499847Z # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4500442Z # Rolling deps
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4500926Z # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4502278Z # All repositories in this file are git-based, using Chromium git mirrors where
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4503554Z # necessary (e.g., a git mirror is used when the source project is SVN-based).
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4504419Z # To update the revision that Chromium pulls for a given dependency:
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4505256Z #
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4505610Z #  # Create and switch to a new branch
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4506094Z #  git new-branch depsroll
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4506752Z #  # Run roll-dep (provided by depot_tools) giving the dep's path and optionally
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4507675Z #  # a regex that will match the line in this file that contains the current
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4508557Z #  # revision. The script ALWAYS rolls the dependency to the latest revision
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4509467Z #  # in origin/master. The path for the dep should start with src/.
2026-09-11T17:23:26.4510202Z #  roll-dep src/third_party/foo_package/src foo_package.git
